{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_XHQDPMxO9qoMYzxjrJWXQcam","type":"tool_result","content":"Web search results for query: \"HWPX mimetype file content application/vnd.hancom.hwpx\"\n\nWeb search results for: **`HWPX mimetype file content application/vnd.hancom.hwpx`**\n\n## Key findings (what the results collectively indicate)\n\n1. **HWPX is a ZIP-based package format**, and inside the `.hwpx` file there is typically a top-level file literally named **`mimetype`** that’s used as a *signature* to identify the package format. ([tech.hancom.com](https://tech.hancom.com/hwpxformat/))  \n2. Multiple Hancom-adjacent/technical sources state that the **content of that internal `mimetype` file is `application/hwp+zip`** (not `application/vnd.hancom.hwpx`). ([forum.developer.hancom.com](https://forum.developer.hancom.com/t/hwp-hwpx-mime-type-whitelist/1641))  \n3. The string **`application/vnd.hancom.hwpx`** appears in the wild mainly as an **extension-to-MIME mapping example** (e.g., Apache `mime.types` / `AddType`), rather than as the “internal package signature” used inside the `.hwpx` container. ([blueyikim.tistory.com](https://blueyikim.tistory.com/434?category=131501))  \n4. Hancom Developer Forum discussion suggests **there isn’t an officially registered MIME type** for HWPX (at least per that thread), and recommends either using `application/hwp+zip` by convention or falling back to `application/octet-stream` for generic download scenarios. ([forum.developer.hancom.com](https://forum.developer.hancom.com/t/hwpx-http-content-type-application-hwp-zip/1752))  \n\n## Notable sources surfaced by the search\n\n### A) HWPX internal structure & the `mimetype` file\n- **Hancom Tech blog: “HWPX 포맷 구조 살펴보기”** — Describes HWPX as a ZIP-structured XML-based format and explicitly explains that the `mimetype` file is used as signature information to distinguish real HWPX packages. ([tech.hancom.com](https://tech.hancom.com/hwpxformat/))  \n- **Hancom Developer Forum: “hwp, hwpx mime type whitelist…”** — States that for `.hwpx`, you can confirm format via the top-level `mimetype` file, and that it contains `application/hwp+zip`. ([forum.developer.hancom.com](https://forum.developer.hancom.com/t/hwp-hwpx-mime-type-whitelist/1641))  \n- **GitHub wiki mirror: “hwpx 내부구조”** — Summarizes the container layout and says the `mimetype` file records `application/hwp+zip`. ([github-wiki-see.page](https://github-wiki-see.page/m/pai-plznw4me/hwp-format-transfer/wiki/hwpx-%EB%82%B4%EB%B6%80%EA%B5%AC%EC%A1%B0))  \n- **hwp-foss Google Group thread (2026)** — Discusses adding HWPX to `shared-mime-info` and notes that the `.hwpx` internal `mimetype` file says `application/hwp+zip`, while also discussing naming/registration concerns. ([groups.google.com](https://groups.google.com/g/hwp-foss/c/8FtrFvVeChk))  \n\n### B) `application/vnd.hancom.hwpx` seen as a web server MIME mapping\n- **Blog post: “[Tips] MIME 타입 추가”** — Gives an Apache configuration example mapping `.hwpx` to `application/vnd.hancom.hwpx` (e.g., `AddType application/vnd.hancom.hwpx .hwpx`). ([blueyikim.tistory.com](https://blueyikim.tistory.com/434?category=131501))  \n\n### C) “What should my HTTP Content-Type be?” discussions\n- **Hancom Developer Forum: “hwpx의 http Content-Type을 application/hwp+zip로 하면 되나요???”** — Says HWPX MIME type is not officially registered; suggests `application/hwp+zip` only if you have a custom agreement/behavior, otherwise `application/octet-stream` is acceptable since it’s a ZIP binary container. ([forum.developer.hancom.com](https://forum.developer.hancom.com/t/hwpx-http-content-type-application-hwp-zip/1752))  \n\n## Practical takeaway (based on the search results)\nIf your goal is **file-content validation** (confirming a file is truly HWPX by inspecting the container), the most consistent answer from the sources above is:\n\n- Open the `.hwpx` as a ZIP\n- Read the top-level `mimetype` file\n- Expect it to contain: **`application/hwp+zip`** ([forum.developer.hancom.com](https://forum.developer.hancom.com/t/hwp-hwpx-mime-type-whitelist/1641))  \n\nWhereas **`application/vnd.hancom.hwpx`** is commonly encountered as a **server-side/extension MIME mapping choice**, not necessarily the internal package signature.
